- **design-ui**: Use this skill when the user asks to design new UI: a screen, flow, page, component, or full product. Triggers: "design a [thing]", "build me a [screen/page/flow]", "create the UI for", "design the [dashboard/settings/onboarding/landing]", "make this screen look [distinctive/professional/not AI]". Dispatches the ui-craft:ui-craft-architect agent, which commits to a distinctive aesthetic POV, generates a token system (OKLCH + variable fonts + modular spacing + spring motion), and produces production-grade UI code that does not look AI-generated, then audits the fresh code with the responsive a… ([TheMizeGuy/ui-craft-public](https://github.com/TheMizeGuy/ui-craft-public/tree/HEAD/skills/design-ui))
